∫ cos πs du = s cosπs.

= c(c+b(1+t-u)) + b u(c+b(1+t-u))
= c^2 + cb(1+t-u)+ b c u + b^2 u(1+t-u)
= c^2 + cb(1+t) + b^2 u(1+t-u) (cb - cb u + bc u cancel).
Integrate u=t to 1 of c^2+cb(1+t) = (1-t)(c^2+cb(1+t)) plus b^2 ∫_t^1 u(1+t-u) du.
Let L=1-t. The integral ∫_t^1 u(1+t-u). Let y=u-t from0 toL, u=t+y, 1+t-u=1-y. Integrand (t+y)(1-y)= t + y - t y - y^2? Actually (t+y)(1-y)=t +y -ty -y^2. Integrate 0 to L: t L + L^2/2 - t L^2/2 - L^3/3. Total h1(t)=L[c^2+cb(1+t)] + b^2[ t L + L^2/2 - t L^2/2 - L^3/3]. Since 1+t =2-L.

Smoothed multiplicative update details:
Input x length n, sum1.
For iteration:
- Compute C = convolve(x, x) using FFT. Ensure nonnegative real; clip tiny neg to 0.
- score=2n*maxC.
- If score < best, update best.
- Compute q. If C has zeros, q= C^α? Since x positive maybe C positive. Use standardized variable u = (C - mean_C)/(maxC - mean_C + eps), but C sum1, mean=1/(2n-1). To focus top, define w = np.exp(beta * (C / maxC - 1))? Since maxC≈scale. Simpler q = C / C.sum(); optionally sharpen: q = np.power(C, α) then normalize. α>1 sharpens, α<1 smooth. For α large, top lags. C values ~1/n; C^α sums scale; okay. Avoid underflow: use log: logC=log(C+eps); q ∝ exp(α logC) = C^α. α float.
- Compute pressure h_i=∑_j x_j q_{i+j} with q normalized sum1? If q is probability over lags, h_i dimension average coordinate ~1/n. For multiplicative gradient Fβ, q normalized; gradient 2h_i. Use η small.
- Update x_new = x * exp(-η * h_i)??? h_i ~1/n; to get O(1) change, η ~ n. Since x_i ~1/n, reducing by factor e^{-η h_i}. If h_i ~1/n, η n. Choose eta = c * n / (max_pressure?) or use step = eta * (h - mean_h) to preserve sum? Since exponential with normalization; if subtract mean, no effect after normalization? Because exp(-η(h - mean)) normalized equivalent up to constant. Centering improves numerical.
Better: normalize x by softmax of negative log? Mirror descent update: x_i ← x_i * exp(-η h_i) / Z. If h_i varies by O(1/n), use η*rng.
Could instead do x_i ← x_i / (1 + η (h_i - min_h)) to avoid zeros. Then normalize.
- Step size adaptation: We can use line search? Try a few step sizes and choose one that reduces current smoothed objective or actual max? Expensive but can evaluate. For each iteration, generate candidates for a few η and α, evaluate score, keep best. This is like random search.

Alternative: Subgradient projected gradient with step size:
x_new = x - α * 2h; set nonnegative; normalize. Since h dimension ~1/n, α ~ n maybe. Use line search over α. Project onto simplex: subtract mean? Detailed functions:
- fft_len(n): 1 << ((2*n-2).bit_length()) but for pressure we need 3n-2; choose next power of two >= max(2n-1, 3n-2) = 3n-2. For n=6733, L=32768. For n=4096, 3n-2=12286 ->16384.

- score_fast(x): C=conv; max=np.max(C); sum=np.sum(x); if sum<0.01 invalid; return 2*n*maxC/sum^2. Let's revisit state-of-art LP in detail. It normalizes f sum = √(2n), M=||f*f||∞. Solve LP max sum g subject to g≥0, f*g ≤ M. Rescale g to sum √(2n), update f ← (1-t)f + t g. Repeating reduces M.
This algorithm likely converges to optimum. We need implement it or approximate.

```
def fft_len_for(n): return 1 << max(1, (3*n-2).bit_length())
def conv_self(x, L=None):
    n=len(x)
    if L is None: L=fft_len_for(n)
    X=np.fft.rfft(x,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(X*X, L)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
For pressure:
```
def pressure(x,q,L):
    n=len(x); M=2*n-1
    X=np.fft.rfft(x,L)
    Q=np.fft.rfft(q[::-1],L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(X*Q, L)
    idx = np.arange(M-1, M-1-n, -1)
    return c[idx]
```

Refine implementation details:
```
def multiplicative_refine(x, deadline, rng, label):
    x=x.copy(); x=np.clip(x,0,1000); s=x.sum(); if s<1e-12 return x,best
    x=x/s
    n=len(x); L=next_power2(3*n-2)
    current_score=None
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       C=conv_self(x,L)
       maxC=C.max(); sumx=x.sum(); score=2*n*maxC/(sumx*sumx) # sumx≈1
       if finite and score<local_best: local_best...
       if score < target+1e-9: break
       # q
       beta = beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*min(1,it/200) # e.g. 5 to 200
       u=C/maxC if maxC>0 else zeros
       logw=beta*(u-1.0)
       logw -= logw.max()
       q=np.exp(logw); q=q/q.sum()
       # maybe active mask top lags? blend with q_smooth
       active_prob=0.2; q = (1-active)*q + active*np.zeros...? not needed.
       h=pressure(x,q,L)
       # ensure h positive? Could be zero.
       h = h - h.min()
       range_h = h.max()
       if range_h < 1e-15: 
           # random perturb, continue with small random
       step_candidates = [0.2,0.5,1,2,4] * (range? )
       # use gamma = a / range_h maybe so exponent range = a for max.
```

Local perturbation function:
```
def block_perturb(x,rng):
  n=x.size
  cand=x.copy()
  for _ in range(rng.integers(1,4)):
    width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//200), max(2,n//4)))
    start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
    sigma=rng.uniform(0.05,0.4)
    cand[start:start+width] *= rng.normal(1.0,sigma,size=width) # or exp
  cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000); cand/=cand.sum()
  return cand
```

```
def _next_pow2(m): return 1 << max(0, int(math.ceil(math.log2(max(1,m))))) if m>1 else 1
```
Simpler:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p*=2
    return p
```

Conv function:
```
def _self_conv(x, L=None):
    n=x.size
    if L is None: L=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    X=np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(X*X, L)
    if c.size < 2*n-1: pad? L>=, okay
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    # remove tiny negative
    c[c<0]=0 (maybe not all? For max negative irrelevant; but for q exp, zeros okay; could clip negative to 0.)
    return c
```
Potential numerical: self convolution of positive real via FFT should be nonnegative; tiny negatives due roundoff. Clip to 0.

Pressure:
```
def _pressure(x,q,L):
    n=x.size; M=2*n-1
    X=np.fft.rfft(x,L); Q=np.fft.rfft(q[::-1],L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(X*Q, L)
    # c may have tiny imag? irfft real
    idx = M-1 - np.arange(n)
    return c[idx]
```
Need ensure L>=n+M-1; yes. For n=1, next_pow2(1)=1; rfft length1 works.

Fast score:
```
def _fast_score(x, L):
    x=np.asarray(x)
    if x.size==0: return inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return inf
    x=np.clip(x,0,1000)
    s=x.sum()
    if s<0.01: return inf
    C=_self_conv(x,L)
    maxC=C.max()
    return float(2*x.size*maxC/(s*s))
```
Since x normalized, use s.

Normalize:
```
def _normalize(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,float).copy()
    x=np.clip(x,0,1000)
    s=x.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: return None
    x/=s
    return x
```
If all values after clip zero, fallback.

```
def _make_linear(n, a=0.5, rev=False):
   if n==1: return np.array([1.0])
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   if rev: t=1-t
   f=1.0 + a*(2*t-1)  # for a>0 increasing if rev false? if a pos, at t=0 1-a, t=1 1+a. if a=0.5 P~0.917. If a=1 f=2t.
   f=np.clip(f,1e-8,None)
   f/=f.sum()
   return f
```

Lowdim objective:
```
def _lowdim_objective(c, B, L):
   z=B.dot(c)
   z -= z.max()
   # if z has nan? c finite. 
   w=np.exp(z)
   s=w.sum()
   if s<=0: return inf
   x=w/s
   return _fast_score(x,L)
```
Coordinate descent:
```
def _lowdim_optimize(n, B, L, starts, deadline, rng):
   best_x=None; best_sc=inf
   for c0 in starts:
      if time>deadline*frac break
      c=c0.copy(); sc=obj(c)
      step=1.0
      no_improve=0
      for dim in range(c.size) random order:
         if time...
         best_delta_sc=sc
         for delta in [-4*step,-2*step,-step,-0.5*step,0.5*step,step,2*step,4*step]:
             c[dim]+=delta; sc2=obj(c); c[dim]-=delta
             if sc2<best_delta_sc: best_delta_sc=sc2; best_delta=delta
         if best_delta !=0:
            c[dim]+=best_delta; sc=best_delta_sc; update best if sc<global
            no_improve=0
         else: no_improve+=1
      if no_improve>=c.size: step*=0.7; if step<0.05: break
```
This is O(D*8*evals). D=6, starts 4 => maybe 4*? If each dimension one best eval per step? The code evaluates 8 deltas per dim each pass. Pass 48 evals. 20 passes=960 evals per start; too many. Implement refine with a `max_seconds` parameter; it checks internal deadline = now+max_seconds. Also uses global deadline maybe min.
```
def _refine(x, max_s, rng, global_deadline):
    t_end=min(global_deadline, time.monotonic()+max_s)
    return _multiplicative_refine(x,t_end,rng)
```

Inside refine, to avoid expensive pressure if only small time left, check per iteration.

Let's detail `_multiplicative_refine`:
```
def _multiplicative_refine(x0, end_time, rng):
    x = _normalize(x0); if None return x0, inf
    best_x=x.copy(); best_sc=_fast_score(x,L)
    current_sc=best_sc
    n=x.size; L=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    it=0; stale=0; last_best=0
    while time.monotonic() < end_time - 1.0:
       it+=1
       C=_self_conv(x,L)
       maxC=C.max(); s=x.sum(); sc=2*n*maxC/(s*s) if s>0 else inf
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best_sc=sc; best_x=x.copy(); last_best=it
       if sc < 1.50274349 - 1e-6: break
       # if current much worse than best, reset
       if sc > best_sc*1.5 and it>20:
           x=best_x.copy(); sc=best_sc; continue
       if it-last_best > 50:
           # random kick to escape
           if rng.random()<0.5:
              z=rng.normal(0,0.2,size=n)
              xn=x*np.exp(z); xn=xn/xn.sum()
              sc2=_fast_score(xn,L)
              if sc2 < best_sc*1.5: x=xn; sc=sc2
           # if still stale and best_sc not much above fallback? break? 
       # q
       beta=20 + 80*min(1,it/200)
       u=C/maxC if maxC>0 else np.ones_like(C)
       logw=beta*(u-1)
       logw-=logw.max(); q=exp(logw); q=q/q.sum()
       # active mask
       thresh=maxC*0.99
       mask=C>=thresh
       if not mask.any(): mask[C.argmax()]=True
       q_act=mask.astype(float); q_act/=q_act.sum()
       q = 0.6*q + 0.4*q_act
       h=_pressure(x,q,L)
       hmin=h.min(); h=h-hmin
       hr=h.max()
       if hr < 1e-15:
           x += rng.normal(0,1e-4,size=n); x=np.clip(x,0,None); x/=x.sum(); continue
       best_trial=None; best_trial_sc=inf
       # maybe if current sc is not best, line search aim best_sc
       for a in (0.5,1.5,4.0):
           gamma=a/hr
           xn=x*np.exp(-gamma*h)
           xn_sum=xn.sum()
           if not finite or xn_sum<=0: continue
           xn/=xn_sum
           # optional tiny uniform
           # evaluate
           sc2=_fast_score(xn,L)
           if sc2 < best_trial_sc:
              best_trial_sc=sc2; best_trial=xn
       # accept if improves best or current
       if best_trial is not None:
           if best_trial_sc < best_sc or best_trial_sc < sc: # current sc
               x=best_trial; sc=best_trial_sc
               if sc < best_sc: last_best=it
               stale=0
           else:
               # occasionally accept small worsening for exploration
               if sc < best_sc*1.02 and rng.random() < 0.1:
                   x=best_trial; sc=best_trial_sc
               else:
                   stale+=1
       # if stale high, make smaller restart from best with kick
       if stale > 30:
           x=best_x.copy(); x*=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,size=n)); x/=x.sum(); sc=_fast_score(x,L); stale=0
    return best_x,best_sc
```

```
def _optimize_raw(raw, deadline, rng, D):
   raw=np.clip(raw.copy(),-8,8)
   sc=eval_raw(raw); best_raw=raw.copy(); best_sc=sc
   step=1.0
   t_last_best=time.monotonic()
   iter_count=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # coordinate pass
       improved=False
       order=rng.permutation(D)
       for d in order:
           if time>deadline: break
           base=raw[d]
           for delta in (-2*step,-step,-0.5*step,0.5*step,step,2*step):
               val=base+delta
               if val<-8 or val>8: continue
               raw[d]=val
               sc2=eval_raw(raw)
               if sc2 < sc:
                   sc=sc2; improved=True
                   if sc2 < best_sc: best_sc=sc2; best_raw=raw.copy(); t_last_best=now
               else:
                   raw[d]=base
           # ensure raw restored? If improved, raw updated. Simpler: evaluate candidates separately and set.
       if not improved:
           step*=0.6
           if step < 0.05:
               # random jump occasionally
               jump_dim=rng.integers(D); raw[jump_dim]+=rng.choice([-2,2])?; raw=clip
               step=max(step,0.5)
       # stochastic acceptance: if no time improve, maybe accept random move not too worse
       if time - t_last_best > 5 and rng.random()<0.3:
           raw2=raw+rng.normal(0,0.5,size=D); clip; sc2=eval; if sc2<best_sc*1.02: raw=raw2; sc=sc2
   return best_raw,best_sc
```

Detailed code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

_TGT = 1.50274349

def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    p=1
    while p < m: p <<=1
    return p
```

Initial random smooth sequences:
```
def _random_smooth(n,rng,D=5):
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   z=np.zeros(n)
   for r in range(1,D+1):
      amp=rng.uniform(0.2,1.5)
      ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
      z += (amp/r)*np.sin(r*np.pi*t + ph)
   x=np.exp(z)
   x/=x.sum()
   return x
```
Sine with endpoint not necessarily zero; okay. Add maybe reverse.

Power sequence:
```
def _power(n,p,rev=False):
 t=np.linspace(0,1,n); if rev t=1-t
 f=(t + 1e-4) ** p
 f/=f.sum(); return f
```
For p large, f(0) small but not zero; endpoint C0 maybe (1e-4*norm)^2. Norm ~1/p, endpoint ~p*1e-4; square ~1/n^2? fine.

```
def eval_raw(raw):
    raw=np.clip(raw,-8,8)
    z=raw - raw.max()
    f=np.exp(z)
    # If f has zeros? no
    x=np.interp(tgrid, kt, f)
    s=x.sum()
    if s<=0: return inf, None
    x/=s
    return _fast_score(x,L), x
```

Potential issue: `np.fft.irfft` output may have tiny imaginary? irfft real. `X*X` should be conjugate symmetric? X from rfft, X*X elementwise; is it the rfft of self-convolution? For real x, FFT of x * x? The rfft of x * x (linear) is pointwise product of FFTs if zero-padded. Since both sequences real, circular convolution via FFT; with zero padding linear. rfft of result? The product of an rfft spectrum with itself may not represent a real even sequence? Actually for full complex FFT, X*X is not Hermitian symmetric in general if x real? X[k] * X[k] not conjugate symmetric; IFFT of X^2 gives real? Since X^2 corresponds to circular convolution of x with x, which is real but not necessarily even. The full IFFT of X^2 is real because it's convolution of real sequences; numerical tiny imag. For rfft, can we use irfft(X*X)? `rfft` only contains nonnegative frequencies. The product `X[k]*X[k]` for k>0 should correspond to the full spectrum of convolution, but is it Hermitian? For convolution of x with x, spectrum = X^2. Since full X is Hermitian: X[-k]=conj(X[k]). Then X^2[-k]=conj(X[k])^2 = conj(X[k]^2). So full spectrum Hermitian, result real. The rfft part X^2 for k≥0 is sufficient; irfft assumes Hermitian. Good.

For pressure convolution x * q_rev, both real; product X*Q rfft part sufficient; result real. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


_TGT = 1.50274349


def _next_pow2(m: int) -> int:
    m = max(1, int(m))
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _self_conv(x: np.ndarray, L: int) -> np.ndarray:
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = x.size
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(X * X, L)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    if c.size and c.min() < 0.0:
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _pressure(x: np.ndarray, q: np.ndarray, L: int) -> np.ndarray:
    n = x.size
    M = 2 * n - 1
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    Q = np.fft.rfft(q[::-1], L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(X * Q, L)
    h = c[M - 1 - np.arange(n)]
    if h.size and h.min() < 0.0:
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    return h


def _fast_score(x: np.ndarray, L: int) -> float:
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.ndim != 1 or x.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return float("inf")
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = _self_conv(x, L)
    mc = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(mc):
        return float("inf")
    return float((2.0 * x.size * mc) / (s * s))


def _normalize(x: np.ndarray):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if x.ndim != 1 or x.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return None
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return None
    x /= s
    return x


def _seq_linear(n: int, a: float = 0.5, rev: bool = False) -> np.ndarray:
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if rev:
        t = 1.0 - t
    f = 1.0 + a * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-12)
    f /= f.sum()
    return f


def _seq_exp(n: int, a: float = 1.0, rev: bool = False) -> np.ndarray:
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if rev:
        t = 1.0 - t
    z = a * (t - 0.5)
    z -= z.max()
    f = np.exp(z)
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-12)
    f /= f.sum()
    return f


def _seq_power(n: int, p: float = 0.8, rev: bool = False) -> np.ndarray:
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if rev:
        t = 1.0 - t
    f = (t + 1e-4) ** p
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-12)
    f /= f.sum()
    return f


def _random_smooth(n: int, rng, D: int = 6) -> np.ndarray:
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for r in range(1, D + 1):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.25, 1.5) / float(r)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        z += amp * np.sin(r * np.pi * t + ph)
    z -= z.max()
    f = np.exp(z)
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-12)
    f /= f.sum()
    return f


def _lowdim_fallback(n: int, raw: np.ndarray, L: int, rng, end_time: float, tgrid, kt) -> np.ndarray:
    # This small helper is not used by the main objective; kept only for clarity/maintenance.
    y = _seq_power(n, 0.75)
    return y


def _optimise_spline(raw0: np.ndarray, end_time: float, rng, D: int, tgrid: np.ndarray,
                     kt: np.ndarray, L: int, update_best):
    raw = np.clip(np.asarray(raw0, dtype=np.float64).copy(), -8.0, 8.0)

    def eval_raw(r):
        r = np.clip(np.asarray(r, dtype=np.float64), -8.0, 8.0)
        z = r - r.max()
        f = np.exp(z)
        f = np.maximum(f, 1e-12)
        x = np.interp(tgrid, kt, f)
        s = float(x.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
            return float("inf"), None
        x /= s
        sc = _fast_score(x, L)
        return sc, x

    sc, x = eval_raw(raw)
    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        return None

    best_raw = raw.copy()
    best_local_sc = sc
    best_local_x = x.copy()
    update_best(best_local_x, best_local_sc)

    step = 1.0
    stale = 0
    last_improve = time.monotonic()
    deltas = (-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0)

    while time.monotonic() < end_time - 0.35:
        improved = False
        order = np.arange(D)
        rng.shuffle(order)
        for d in order:
            if time.monotonic() >= end_time - 0.35:
                break
            old = raw[d]
            cur_best_val = old
            cur_best_sc = sc
            cur_best_x = None

            for dm in deltas:
                val = old + dm * step
                if val < -8.0 or val > 8.0:
                    continue
                cand = raw.copy()
                cand[d] = val
                cand_sc, cand_x = eval_raw(cand)
                if math.isfinite(cand_sc) and cand_sc < cur_best_sc - 1e-14:
                    cur_best_sc = cand_sc
                    cur_best_val = val
                    cur_best_x = cand_x
                    if cand_sc < best_local_sc:
                        best_local_sc = cand_sc
                        best_local_raw = cand.copy()
                        if cur_best_x is not None:
                            best_local_x = cur_best_x.copy()
                        last_improve = time.monotonic()
                        update_best(best_local_x, best_local_sc)

            if cur_best_val != old:
                raw[d] = cur_best_val
                sc = cur_best_sc
                improved = True

        if not improved:
            stale += 1
            step *= 0.72
            if step < 0.05:
                step = 0.25
                if rng.random() < 0.6:
                    d1 = int(rng.integers(0, D))
                    d2 = int(rng.integers(0, D)) if D > 1 else d1
                    raw[d1] = np.clip(raw[d1] + rng.choice((-2.0, 2.0)), -8.0, 8.0)
                    if D > 1 and d2 != d1:
                        raw[d2] = np.clip(raw[d2] + rng.choice((-1.0, 1.0)), -8.0, 8.0)
                    st_sc, st_x = eval_raw(raw)
                    if math.isfinite(st_sc) and st_x is not None and st_sc < 2.2:
                        sc, x = st_sc, st_x
                    step = 1.0
        else:
            stale = 0
            if rng.random() < 0.25:
                step *= 0.9

        if time.monotonic() - last_improve > 8.0 and rng.random() < 0.25:
            rd = int(rng.integers(0, D))
            raw[rd] = np.clip(raw[rd] + rng.normal(0.0, 1.25), -8.0, 8.0)
            r_sc, r_x = eval_raw(raw)
            if math.isfinite(r_sc) and r_x is not None and r_sc < best_local_sc * 1.04:
                sc, x = r_sc, r_x

    if best_local_x is not None:
        update_best(best_local_x, best_local_sc)
    return best_local_x


def _multiplicative_refine(x0: np.ndarray, end_time: float, rng, L: int):
    x = _normalize(x0)
    if x is None:
        return x0, float("inf")
    n = int(x.size)
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_sc = _fast_score(x, L)
    if not math.isfinite(best_sc):
        ln = _seq_linear(n, 0.5)
        ln = _normalize(ln)
        best_x = ln
        best_sc = _fast_score(ln, L)
        x = ln.copy()

    it = 0
    stale = 0
    last_best_it = 0
    current_sc = best_sc

    while time.monotonic() < end_time - 0.6:
        it += 1

        c = _self_conv(x, L)
        maxc = float(c.max())
        s = float(x.sum())
        if maxc <= 0.0 or s <= 0.0:
            break
        sc = float((2.0 * n * maxc) / (s * s))
        current_sc = sc

        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_x = x.copy()
            last_best_it = it

        # Restart if we have wandered too far from the local best.
        if (it - last_best_it > 70) or (math.isfinite(best_sc) and sc > best_sc * 1.6):
            x = best_x.copy()
            if rng.random() < 0.8:
                x *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, size=n))
                x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
                sx = float(x.sum())
                if sx > 0.0:
                    x /= sx
            sc = _fast_score(x, L)
            current_sc = sc
            stale = max(0, stale - 10)
            continue

        # Dual weights: smooth exponential of the current profile, plus the near-active lags.
        beta = 10.0 + 90.0 * min(1.0, it / 180.0)
        u = c / maxc
        logw = beta * (u - 1.0)
        logw -= logw.max()
        q = np.exp(logw)
        qsum = float(q.sum())
        if qsum <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(qsum):
            q = np.ones_like(c)
            qsum = float(q.sum())
        q /= qsum

        active = c >= (0.99 * maxc)
        if not active.any():
            active[int(c.argmax())] = True
        qa = active.astype(np.float64)
        qa /= float(qa.sum())
        q = 0.60 * q + 0.40 * qa

        h = _pressure(x, q, L)
        hmin = float(h.min())
        h = h - hmin
        hr = float(h.max())
        if hr < 1e-15:
            # If the current point is degenerate, add a very small symmetric-free jitter.
            x += rng.normal(0.0, 1e-5, size=n)
            x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
            sx = float(x.sum())
            if sx > 0.0:
                x /= sx
            continue

        best_trial = None
        best_trial_sc = float("inf")
        gammas = (0.75 / hr, 2.0 / hr, 5.0 / hr)

        for gamma in gammas:
            xn = x * np.exp(-gamma * h)
            xn = np.maximum(xn, 1e-13)
            # Add a negligible full-support mass so coordinates do not become permanently dead.
            xn = (1.0 - 1e-5) * xn + (1e-5) * (1.0 / float(n))
            sx = float(xn.sum())
            if sx <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(sx):
                continue
            xn /= sx
            tsc = _fast_score(xn, L)
            if math.isfinite(tsc) and tsc < best_trial_sc:
                best_trial_sc = tsc
                best_trial = xn

        accepted = False
        if best_trial is not None:
            # Accept moves that improve the incumbent, or that improve the current walk and are
            # not catastrophically worse than the incumbent.
            if best_trial_sc < best_sc - 1e-12 or best_trial_sc < current_sc - 1e-12:
                x = best_trial
                current_sc = best_trial_sc
                if current_sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = current_sc
                    best_x = x.copy()
                    last_best_it = it
                stale = 0
                accepted = True
            elif current_sc < best_sc * 1.02 and rng.random() < 0.22:
                x = best_trial
                current_sc = best_trial_sc
                accepted = True

        if not accepted:
            stale += 1
            if stale > 45:
                x = best_x.copy()
                if rng.random() < 0.8:
                    x *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, size=n))
                    x = np.maximum(x, 1e-13)
                    sx = float(x.sum())
                    if sx > 0.0:
                        x /= sx
                current_sc = _fast_score(x, L)
                stale = 0

    if best_x is None:
        best_x = x
    return best_x, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    if not math.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 1300.0
    hard_end = t0 + budget
    work_end = t0 + max(0.0, budget - 12.0)
    if work_end < hard_end - 2.0:
        work_end = hard_end - 1.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Use the supplied parent as a valid anchor and source of length.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(p.sum()) > 0.0:
                parent = p.copy()
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        n = int(parent.size)
    else:
        n = 2048
    if n < 64:
        n = 512
    L = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    best_x = None
    best_sc = float("inf")
    candidates = []

    def update_best(x, sc):
        nonlocal best_x, best_sc
        y = _normalize(x)
        if y is None or not math.isfinite(sc):
            return
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_x = y.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc)

    def consider(x):
        y = _normalize(x)
        if y is None:
            return
        sc = _fast_score(y, L)
        update_best(y, sc)
        candidates.append((sc, y.copy()))
        if len(candidates) > 36:
            candidates.sort(key=lambda zz: zz[0])
            del candidates[24:]

    # Quick deterministic and random smooth initialisation.
    if parent is not None:
        consider(parent)

    for a in (0.20, 0.35, 0.50, 0.70, 0.90, 1.00):
        consider(_seq_linear(n, a, False))
        consider(_seq_linear(n, a, True))

    for a in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        consider(_seq_exp(n, a, False))
        consider(_seq_exp(n, a, True))

    for p in (0.4, 0.6, 0.8, 1.2, 1.8, 2.5):
        consider(_seq_power(n, p, False))
        consider(_seq_power(n, p, True))

    for _ in range(10):
        consider(_random_smooth(n, rng, D=rng.integers(4, 9)))

    # If everything went wrong, provide a safe non-symmetric anchor.
    if best_x is None or not math.isfinite(best_sc):
        fb = _seq_linear(n, 0.5)
        fb = _normalize(fb)
        best_x = fb
        best_sc = _fast_score(fb, L)

    # Low-dimensional spline search.  This comparatively cheap phase helps the full-dimensional
    # refinement start in an asymmetric basin instead of the symmetric continuum optimum.
    if time.monotonic() < work_end - 5.0:
        D = 10 if n >= 800 else 8
        tgrid = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        kt = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, D)

        raw_starts = []

        def add_raw(raw):
            if raw is None:
                return
            raw = np.clip(np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64), -8.0, 8.0)
            raw -= np.median(raw)
            raw = np.clip(raw, -8.0, 8.0)
            for r in raw_starts:
                if float(np.mean(np.abs(r - raw))) < 1e-4:
                    return
            raw_starts.append(raw)

        for a in (0.35, 0.55, 0.80, 1.00, 1.25):
            for rev in (False, True):
                tt = kt
                if rev:
                    tt = 1.0 - tt
                f = 1.0 + a * (2.0 * tt - 1.0)
                f = np.maximum(f, 1e-6)
                add_raw(np.log(f) - np.mean(np.log(f)))

        for a in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
            add_raw(a * (kt - 0.5))
            add_raw(-a * (kt - 0.5))

        for p in (0.5, 0.8, 1.2, 2.0):
            add_raw(p * np.log(np.maximum(kt, 1e-4)))

        if best_x is not None:
            fv = np.interp(kt, tgrid, best_x)
            fv = np.maximum(fv, 1e-10)
            add_raw(np.log(fv) - np.mean(np.log(fv)))

        if len(raw_starts) < 4:
            add_raw(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=D))

        # Score starts and keep the most promising few.
        scored_starts = []
        for r in raw_starts:
            rr = np.clip(r, -8.0, 8.0)
            z = rr - rr.max()
            f = np.exp(z)
            f = np.maximum(f, 1e-12)
            x = np.interp(tgrid, kt, f)
            sx = float(x.sum())
            if sx > 0:
                x /= sx
                sc = _fast_score(x, L)
            else:
                sc = float("inf")
            if math.isfinite(sc):
                scored_starts.append((sc, r.copy()))
        scored_starts.sort(key=lambda zz: zz[0])

        remaining = max(0.0, work_end - time.monotonic())
        if remaining > 20.0 and scored_starts:
            n_starts = min(3, len(scored_starts))
            # Spend a disciplined fraction of the budget here, leaving most time for exact-domain polishing.
            total_spline = min(170.0, remaining * 0.30)
            per_start = max(12.0, total_spline / max(1, n_starts))
            for i in range(n_starts):
                end_i = min(work_end - 4.0, time.monotonic() + per_start)
                if end_i - time.monotonic() < 5.0:
                    break
                _optimise_spline(scored_starts[i][1], end_i, rng, D, tgrid, kt, L, update_best)

    # Full-dimensional multiplicative refinement from a few diverse incumbent points.
    if time.monotonic() < work_end - 8.0:
        cand_sorted = sorted([(sc, x) for sc, x in candidates if math.isfinite(sc)], key=lambda zz: zz[0])
        starts = []
        for sc, x in cand_sorted:
            if len(starts) >= 4:
                break
            distinct = True
            for y in starts:
                if x is not None and float(np.mean(np.abs(x - y))) < 2e-3:
                    distinct = False
                    break
            if distinct and x is not None:
                starts.append(x.copy())

        rand_start = _random_smooth(n, rng, D=rng.integers(4, 8))
        sc_rand = _fast_score(rand_start, L)
        if math.isfinite(sc_rand) and sc_rand < best_sc * 1.15 and sc_rand < 2.25:
            distinct = True
            for y in starts:
                if float(np.mean(np.abs(rand_start - y))) < 2e-3:
                    distinct = False
                    break
            if distinct:
                starts.append(rand_start.copy())

        if not starts:
            starts.append(_seq_linear(n, 0.5))

        starts = starts[:3]
        remaining = max(0.0, work_end - time.monotonic() - 25.0)
        if remaining > 20.0 and starts:
            per_refine = max(45.0, remaining / max(1, len(starts)))
            per_refine = min(420.0, per_refine)
            for x0 in starts:
                if time.monotonic() >= work_end - 25.0:
                    break
                end_i = min(work_end - 25.0, time.monotonic() + per_refine)
                if end_i - time.monotonic() < 8.0:
                    break
                xb, scb = _multiplicative_refine(x0, end_i, rng, L)
                update_best(xb, scb)

    # Short final discrete local perturbations around the best exact-objective candidate.
    local_end = work_end
    if best_x is not None and time.monotonic() < local_end - 2.0:
        base = np.clip(best_x, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()
        while time.monotonic() < local_end - 2.0:
            x0 = np.maximum(base, 1e-13).copy()
            nblk = int(rng.integers(1, 3))
            for _ in range(nblk):
                w = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 1000), max(2, n // 32)))
                s = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
                sig = float(rng.uniform(0.04, 0.22))
                x0[s:s + w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, size=w))
            x0 = np.maximum(x0, 0.0)
            sx = float(x0.sum())
            if sx > 0.0 and math.isfinite(sx):
                x0 /= sx
                sc = _fast_score(x0, L)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    base = x0.copy()
                    best_x = x0.copy()
                elif rng.random() < 0.02 and sc < best_sc * 1.005:
                    base = x0.copy()

    # Final exact acceptance: use the authoritative evaluator on the last candidate.
    if best_x is None:
        best_x = _seq_linear(n, 0.5)
    best_x = _normalize(best_x)
    if best_x is None:
        best_x = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    final_list = [float(v) for v in np.clip(best_x, 0.0, 1000.0)]
    try:
        if not final_list or not all(math.isfinite(v) for v in final_list):
            raise ValueError("non-finite list")
        final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
    except Exception:
        final_score = float("inf")

    # Very conservative fallback if the final exact evaluation somehow rejects the candidate.
    if not math.isfinite(final_score) or final_score > 2.5:
        fb = _seq_linear(n, 0.45)
        fb = _normalize(fb)
        if fb is None:
            fb = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        fb_list = [float(v) for v in np.clip(fb, 0.0, 1000.0)]
        try:
            fb_score = float(evaluate_sequence(fb_list))
        except Exception:
            fb_score = float("inf")
        if (not math.isfinite(final_score)) or (math.isfinite(fb_score) and fb_score < final_score):
            final_list = fb_list

    # Ensure we have a valid finite non-empty list before the sandbox deadline.
    if not final_list:
        final_list = [1.0]
    final_out = []
    for v in final_list:
        if math.isfinite(v):
            final_out.append(float(v))
    if not final_out:
        final_out = [1.0]
    return final_out
```
